  model turn start (start): sent [R, T2]
  start: real Native turn armed F (f2) with timer R = c9_0/subject_1#2.0000 due 1050 and F2 (f3) with timer T2 due 1100
  park: R(seq 1100, native.timer, released -, seen 0, done True, kept Arrived)
  open: recover opens generation 1; ParkRecord.ResolutionGeneration = 1; C0 = kernel.drain seq 800
  arrival: A(seq 900, native.cancel, released -, seen 0, done False, kept -) from sender 0/other, withheld while the recover drain is open
  C0 page: restored [R] with ReleasedBy (1,1); cursor -> R; gate row R; sends C = kernel.drain seq 950
  marks at epoch 1: R(seq 1100, native.timer, released (1,1), seen 1, done False, kept -); A(seq 900, native.cancel, released -, seen 1, done False, kept -); C(seq 950, kernel.drain, released -, seen 1, done False, kept -)
  deliver C (order: [C, R])
  C: no kept row past the cursor and gate row R taken up (seen 1 = epoch 1): Complete clears the resolution
  retake by the same process after Complete committed, before R reached a model turn: new owner at epoch 2 rebuilds from durable rows only (memory, holds and polled rows are lost)
  poll at epoch 2 (limit 500): [A, R]
  deliver A (order: [A, R])
  model turn A (native.cancel): end Cancelled; recalled [R, T2]
  model inputs: [A]
  rows: R recalled by A's turn (never reached the model); T2 recalled by A's turn (never reached the model); C0 consumed (drain turn); A consumed by a model turn; C consumed (drain turn)
  instance: status Cancelled, resolution cleared, ResolutionGeneration 1, cohort mark none
  assertions:
    FAIL F completions (records c:f2:F): 0 (expected 1)
    FAIL actual model transitions after the start: [A native.cancel -> end Cancelled] (expected [R native.timer -> c:f2:F ; A native.cancel -> end Cancelled])
    PASS F2 still waiting when A reached the model: True (expected True)
    PASS F2 completions: 0 (expected 0)
    PASS instance ended: Cancelled (expected Cancelled)
    PASS drain completed: True (expected True)
    FAIL R's fate: recalled by A's turn (never reached the model) (expected consumed by a model turn)
    PASS undone rows remaining: 0 (expected 0)
  real prototype calls: NativeModel.TurnAsync x2
RESULT FR-1/takeover-after-complete rules=negative: FAIL
